| I-79 | K-C — THE BOOKS/LEDGER FIX, increment 3 of the "drop-in Component architecture" (owner-ruled 2026-08-03, verbatim intent): the ledger, when opened, shows **P&L on the LEFT page, Balance Sheet on the RIGHT page — an open two-page book spread, both visible**; **zooming in enters READING mode of the SELECTED report (P&L default); in reading mode LEFT/RIGHT moves to the other report, the SAME as the other reading views**; **all transitions are SMOOTH animations**. **THIS ROW SUPERSEDES I-74** (the batch-1 single-panel ledger) ON THE RECORD, never silently — see the supersession breakdown at the end. **(1) THE CLOSED BOOK carries forward from I-74 unchanged** — `ledgerBook()` a thick cover mesh lying FLAT (`rotation.x=−π/2`), placement `bound{surface:'seat-0',region:'front'}` at world (−420,2,560), `userData.ledger` the open-click marker, registered `focusGroups['ledger']`; a click still FLIPS it open. **(2) OPEN = a TWO-PAGE SPREAD (NEW `utilization/bench/src/ledger-spread.ts`, 180 ln)** — on open the closed book flips into a spread standing vertical in front of the seat (anchor = the book's world pos + (0,138,0)): a LEFT page (world-x < right) rendering BOOKS_PANEL filled with the **P&L**, a RIGHT page rendering BOOKS_PANEL filled with the **REAL Balance Sheet**. Each page is a SEPARATE `THREE.Group` **SCENE-PLACED** (`scene.add`, NOT camera-parented — the I-74 camera-overlay+veil is RETIRED) and **FOCUSABLE** (`focusGroups['ledger-pnl']` / `['ledger-balance']`, so the camera read-ladder targets each page) built via `layoutFace(BOOKS_PANEL,…)` over an OPAQUE backing sheet (MeshBasicMaterial transparent:false, depth-writing → occludes the table; the I-70 discipline realized as plain-opaque since the scene-placed design has NO veil to sort over). **(3) THE FILLS (I-56d honored)** — the RIGHT page is REAL from the projection: `computeBalance` = Assets = cash + Σ receivables(holder=seat) · Liabilities = Σ debts(debtor=seat) · Equity = assets − liabilities; the footnote speaks the identity (kept byte-stable for the gate oracle). The LEFT (P&L) page is an HONEST bracketed placeholder — the slice has no revenue/COGS (I-56d) so every figure reads `[—]`, NOTHING invented (this DISCHARGES I-74's single `[P&L — next increment]` tab-label into a full honest P&L page; still zero invented figures). **(4) ZOOM-TO-READ = fit-to-frame of the SELECTED page (the "stuck zoomed" fix)** — `readSelected()` calls camera.ts `readView('ledger-'+selected)` (default 'pnl'), and on open `setLastFocus('ledger-pnl')` so the EXISTING wheel organic-read-entry (camera.ts, unedited) enters the P&L page's fit pose; `readView`/`fitAlong` put ALL EIGHT bbox corners inside NDC — not cropped/over-zoomed. **(5) LEFT/RIGHT SWITCH = a smooth glide to the other page (reuses the EXISTING pan gesture + read machinery, ZERO shared-file edit)** — the harness pointer handler already routes read-mode drags to `cam.panBy` (which clamps the look within the current page); `tickSpread()` (run each frame via the component's `tick` → `tickLedger`) watches `readState()` + `lookAtPoint().x`, and when the look is panned past a threshold TOWARD the other page (`> pnlPage.x + SWITCH_T` from P&L → Balance; `< balPage.x − SWITCH_T` from Balance → P&L) it calls `readView` on the other page — a smooth camera glide. Anti-oscillation is STATE-based: the switch is gated on `!gliding()`, so after a switch the look glides to the new page centre (offset falls back under threshold) before another switch can fire — no clock. **(6) SMOOTH (waits on STATE, never clocks — I-60f)** — the closed→spread OPEN is a per-frame ease-out lerp (`openAmt` 0→1: the pages swing apart from the anchor + ease up to full scale, settling ~15 frames, `spreadSettled()` the STATE the gate waits on); the zoom-to-read and the left/right switch are the camera glide (`tickGlide`, lerp 0.1). **RECORDED DEVIATION FROM THE PLAN (I-58, not silent):** the plan said "Use ctx.theater (readView/…)", i.e. add `readView` to the ctx seam (a game3d.ts + component.ts edit). | I-218 | THE ROUND WORLD (owner-ruled, the sphere ask honored at the horizon at last): the COUNTERTOP is a DISC — CylinderGeometry at COUNTER_RADIUS = ringRadius + 540 (the ring, the box's slot and bulk, margin), top face still the y=0 resting plane (the strata law untouched); the 2600×2200 square slab retired. The backdrop's haze disc hugs the round counter — ONE circular horizon; the visible edge of the world is the spherical bound's equator and 'the edge of the screen' is a circle from every zoom. The I-158 sphere ruling now has its visible body, not just its math. | benign | The world finally looks like its own geometry: a circle to its edge, no corners anywhere. |
| I-219 | THE AREA CAMERA ≡ THE SEAT CAMERA (owner-specified, exact): for seat-area scenics the camera takes the SEAT preset's own y elevation (≈622 at zoom 2.2) and keeps the SAME gap from the area's OUTER edge that the seat cam keeps from the board's outer face (≈250) — BOTH measured LIVE (mapPreset('seat-0') + the live board's bounding sphere), never hard numbers, so the equality survives every future scale/ring tune; symmetric across all six seats by the preset law's own symmetry; the look stays 0,0,0 (the anchor law). The 35°-to-center default remains for every non-area scenic (box, table). | benign | The area view is the seat view's sibling by construction — same height, same breathing room, measured not typed. |
| I-220 | THE ZOOM LAW, THE OWNER'S FINAL GRAMMAR (rule 5 DELETED — 'it's in direct conflict with the zone rule'; the board zone is JUST a zone centered at 0,0,0, any zone's center may be displaced x,y,z — six zones in a 2×3 would each carry their own): ONE RECURSIVE RULE — a ZONE is an object zoomable to 80% OF THE FRAME, NO MORE; its CHILDREN anchor by click and zoom to THEIR 80% (deeper than the zone's 100%); ONE MORE NOTCH past a child's 80% SLIPS INTO READ; read exits ONE STEP (wheel-out or a plain click — never a drag-end) back to the anchor's 80%; EMPTY SPACE IS UNZOOMABLE ('you cannot zoom into a zone area unless there's something there to anchor to' — the folder alone means the folder is the only door); zones change by CLICK, never scroll. Implemented verbatim: fitDist(focus, 0.8) sphere-fit is the one wall measure; zoneAnchorOf the one containment map; the scenic fill re-tuned to 0.8 so buttons land ON the wall; exitReadStep wired to the click pipeline ahead of re-anchoring; the old four-rung ladder (I-66b) SUPERSEDED on the record. Gates that call readView/glideTo directly are untouched; wheel-driving drills re-derive at the next battery pass (recorded). | benign | One rule, applied recursively: eighty percent, then the child, then the page — and nothing in between to get lost in. |
| I-221 | AREA-BUTTON COMPOSITION (owner-tuned, superseding the I-219 seat-match): elevation = HALF the seat cam's (≈311), and THE AREA'S NEAREST EDGE FILLS THE ENTIRE BOTTOM EDGE OF THE VIEW — the composition rule outranks look-at-center for area buttons: the pull-back derives from the edge's width against the horizontal FOV (slant = (W/2)/tan(fovH/2)), the pitch sets the near edge exactly on the frame's bottom (atan(h/b) − fovV/2), and the look lands where that axis meets the felt toward center. All live-measured (the surface's own corners), symmetric across seats. | benign | The table starts at your fingertips: the near edge IS the bottom of the screen. |
| I-222 | THE OWNER'S SCREENSHOT VERDICT on I-221 ('does area 0 look like the bottom of the play area fills the bottom of the screen?' — no): TWO causes, both fixed. (1) the look derivation divided by tan(pitch) with a clamp path that could shoot the gaze NEAR-LEVEL (the screenshot's high horizon) — the axis is now a pure DIRECTION vector (pitch floored at 0.12 rad, no tan, no singularity). (2) THE AREA WAS INVISIBLE — the surface rendered at 10% opacity with no edge, so even a correct composition read as empty felt: opacity → 0.22 + an EDGE OUTLINE, giving the composition rule an actual edge to fill the bottom with (I-162 readability, not decoration). | benign | You cannot fill the frame's bottom with an edge nobody can see. |
| I-223 | THE OWNER'S A/B DIAGNOSTIC ('area 1 DOES fit!' while area 0 did not — and seat 1 is the ANCHORED, axis-aligned seat): the I-221 composition measured the surface via its world AABB, which is EXACT for the unrotated seat and INFLATED for every yawed one (a rotated rectangle's axis-aligned box is bigger than the rectangle) — oversized width → oversized pull-back → the area-0 sliver. FIX: the surface's size comes from ITS LAW (seat-grid's surfaceSize — exact at every yaw); the near edge = center + half-depth along the outward radial by the surface's own construction. The lesson is I-215's sphere lesson again, one level down: never measure a KNOWN shape through an axis-aligned proxy — ask the shape. | benign | Seat 1 told on the bounding box: the law knew the size all along. |
| I-224 | THE BOX JOINS THE AREA COMPOSITION (owner-asked, sealing the arc with his own vindication: 'See? That's why everything sits inside a sphere — as I was visualizing it in my head'): the box button takes the SAME camera law as the play areas — half elevation, the near edge on the frame's bottom — with its width/near-edge measured from its BOUNDING SPHERE (rotation-safe; the AABB already lied once at I-223); the areas keep their grid-law truth. The ring-citizen family now shares one composition: areas by their grids, the box by its sphere, both under the same pitch/width construction. | benign | The sphere he visualized is now the measure the camera trusts. |
| I-225 | THE AABB'S THIRD LIE (owner: 'seat 0 read pulls back too far, seat 1 perfect — same issue, for sure' — his A/B nailed the class before I did): BOTH read fits still measured the world axis-aligned box — exact at the anchored seat, inflated at every yawed one. FIX: the seat-AREA overhead read fits the GRID'S OWN size (surfaceSize against both FOV axes ×1.08); the FACE-ON read (boards, sheets, obj cards) fits the bounding SPHERE (fill 0.9 × the F-7 small-object factor). fitAlong survives only where geometry is axis-aligned by construction (the fixed table's overhead — 'PERFECT' per the owner, untouched). NOTE for the battery: the VG8c seat-read pose pins re-derive at the next pass (seat-1 'perfect' is the owner's spec; the formula changed under it). The class is now EXTINCT in camera.ts: no fit measures an AABB of anything that can rotate. | benign | Third strike for the bounding box; the sphere and the grid now measure everything that turns. |
